# Skills · Q · part 13

833 entries, qmd to qqbot-channel. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **qmd**: How to install, configure, and manage the qmd local search MCP server. Covers Node.js installation, npm install of @tobilu/qmd, registering with claude mcp add, adding and re-indexing collections with qmd collection add / qmd update / qmd embed, and diagnosing MCP server registration issues. Use this skill whenever setting up qmd on a new machine, adding a new qmd collection, re-indexing after file changes, or debugging why qmd tools are not appearing in a session. ([jfcrenshaw/dotfiles](https://github.com/jfcrenshaw/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/claude/skills/qmd))
- **qmd**: Indexes local notes, docs, and transcripts with qmd hybrid retrieval (BM25 + vectors + LLM rerank) via @tobilu/qmd. Use when searching a personal knowledge base semantically without cloud APIs. Not for Obsidian vault editing (obsidian), Mesh Memory MCP worklogs (mesh-memory), or arXiv paper search (arxiv). ([Kayforkind/skill-slice](https://github.com/Kayforkind/skill-slice/tree/HEAD/skills/qmd))
- **qmd**: Index and search local markdown/text/code knowledge bases with the qmd CLI (tobi/qmd, "Quick Markdown Search") - a local hybrid search engine with BM25 keyword search, vector search, and LLM reranking. Use this skill whenever the user wants to search notes, docs, journals, or a codebase semantically; index a new folder or dataset; update or re-index an existing qmd index; set up a project-local or named index; or install qmd. Trigger on words like "qmd", "index my notes", "search my knowledge base", "semantic search over these files", "find in my docs". ([ksinica/skills](https://github.com/ksinica/skills/tree/HEAD/qmd))
- **qmd**: Quick Markdown Search - Full-text and vector search for markdown and JSONL files. Use for searching conversation history, documentation, and any text-based collections. Triggers on "search for", "find in sessions", "query history". ([lev-os/lev-content](https://github.com/lev-os/lev-content/tree/HEAD/sources/skills/lev-find-qmd))
- **qmd**: Bootstrap QMD search instructions from the installed qmd CLI. Use when users ask to find notes, retrieve documents, inspect a wiki, or answer from indexed local markdown. ([LevelInteractive/level-ai-obsidian](https://github.com/LevelInteractive/level-ai-obsidian/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/qmd) · 8 copies)
- **qmd**: Search personal knowledge base using QMD (Query Markup Documents). Use when users ask to find information, search notes, look up details about house/family/kids/work, recall stored facts, or retrieve any previously saved knowledge. Also use when adding new information to remember. ([luanzeba/dotfiles](https://github.com/luanzeba/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/skills/qmd))

- **qmd**: Use for local hybrid search across markdown notes, documentation, and knowledge bases. Ideal for finding related content, retrieving documents from indexed collections, or searching personal notes. ([MrGKanev/agent-skills](https://github.com/MrGKanev/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/qmd))

Indexes any text-based source (TypeScript, JavaScript, Python, Go, Rust, JSON, plus markdown/notes) when collection patterns include those extensions. - **qq**: The question queue for coding agents. Use BEFORE asking the human anything — a decision, a permission, or an action only they can do — and whenever a qq reminder lists waiting cards.
